**1. Platform selection before any probe (findings 1 and 4).**

Keep `Result<DshSelection, DshUnselected>` and the successful selection's
independent `Result<DshSeams, CompositeError>`. Keep the override priority
`BROKKR_DSH_BIN`, then `FORGE_DSH_BIN`, then `dsh`. A failed override never
tries the next source. NUL refuses by name before filesystem access, home work
or probing. Preserve OS path bytes through lookup; unrepresentable canonical
paths retain their explicit refusal. `Safe` is output-only, never lookup input.

Use private target-specific lookup functions in `composite.rs`. On Unix the
only direct-path classifier is a literal `/`; backslash, drive-like spelling,
spaces and extensions are ordinary filename bytes. The selected-path guard
uses this same predicate or consumes established selection directly, rather
than implementing another character test. A successful selection is reused
for version and core discovery; no bare-name retry occurs.

Capture the actual lookup context once. `None` PATH consults the target libc's
default-search rule; on the observed GNU target use the platform `_CS_PATH`
value through a read-only binding, with native-positive tests establishing
agreement. Do not hardcode the observed `/bin:/usr/bin`, inherit a removed
parent PATH, or launch `getconf`, a shell or `which`. Other Unix targets must
use their own libc rule, not a GNU constant. Present PATH is split without
losing ordered empty entries: `Some("")` is not `None`. No-match diagnostics
name unsuccessful default search or explicit PATH search as appropriate.
The [Rust Command contract](https://doc.rust-lang.org/std/process/struct.Command.html#method.new)
and [glibc lookup source](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/bminor/glibc/master/posix/execvpe.c)
support this distinction; the actual target's native child remains the oracle.

Windows is a separate implementation of Rust's executable selection, not a
Unix PATH loop or cmd/PATHEXT search. For the supported Rust behavior, search
an explicitly supplied child PATH, application directory, system directory,
Windows directory, then parent PATH; preserve whether child PATH was changed.
Omitted child PATH does not remove parent search. Skip empty Windows PATH
entries as Rust does. Use Windows path and suffix rules: a bare filename with
no dot searches with `.exe`; an explicit path without an `.exe` suffix tries
the appended suffix before the literal path. Do not turn a terminal launch
error into a later candidate. The
[Rust 1.88 implementation](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/rust-lang/rust/1.88.0/library/std/src/sys/process/windows.rs)
provides the concrete baseline; validate on the candidate compiler as well as
MSRV because std's details can change. This is an implementation decision,
not a claim of Windows execution. Canonicalization must preserve the exact
selected target, including drive-relative/UNC semantics, or refuse by cause.

| Lookup/observation state | Probe authority and report |
|---|---|
| Native default search or explicit PATH finds a proven candidate | One exact selected DSH path; home remains independent. |
| Native no-match, including absent-PATH names missing from default search | No target or DSH/Node probe; named search failure, with absence only context. |
| NUL, terminal error, unsupported loader evidence or unrepresentable path | No target; safely render declaration, candidate where relevant and precise cause. |
| Selected executable, missing home | Its version may be reported beside named home/composite refusal. |
| Selected executable, version fails | Report that selected failure; no alternative candidate or composite observation. |
| Selected DSH, Node selection refuses | No Node probe; composite unreadable with its selection cause. |

**2. Admission evidence and the exact loader-refusal boundary.**

Keep `Candidate::{Admitted, Passed, Refused}`. `Passed` means a demonstrated
native continuation cause, not a collapsed error. Preserve terminal errors
such as ELOOP and distinguish accumulated permission failure from true
NotFound. A missing prerequisite of an existing executable is `Refused`, not
absence of that candidate. A refusal says which candidate, which prerequisite
and whether it is missing, invalid or cannot be established; generic selected-
binary-not-found after spawning is too late.

Replace `interpreter_obstruction`'s catch-all success with bounded format
inspection on the opened candidate. Read the necessary headers/tables with
checked offsets, checked counts and target-format bounds, never load whole
arbitrary binaries or trust length fields for allocation. Keep visited
canonical interpreter identities and a bounded interpreter-chain depth;
cycles/depth exhaustion are named unprovable outcomes. Retain inspected bytes
that later feed identity within this observation rather than rereading them.

The chosen admission cases are explicit:

| Form | Required nonexecuting evidence |
|---|---|
| Linux native ELF on the target architecture | Validate class, byte order, version, executable/shared-image type, machine, header/table sizes and file ranges. Inspect the complete bounded program-header table and its load segments, including filesz/memsz and arithmetic validity. A dynamic image needs its unique, well-formed NUL-terminated `PT_INTERP` path, opened and checked as a loadable target ELF interpreter with valid program headers/segments. An ordinary static image has no interpreter prerequisite. Unsupported architecture/ABI, malformed/truncated tables or an unproved interpreter form refuse specifically. Magic or an existing loader file alone never admits. |
| Unix script | Parse the shebang using that target's kernel limit and delimiter rules. Inspect the named absolute interpreter through the native-image checks, following supported script-interpreter links with cycle/depth protection. A missing loader behind an executable interpreter is a refusal at the original candidate with the chain named. Relative/ambiguous/unsupported interpreter forms refuse, never guess shell behavior. |
| Measured `#!/usr/bin/env node` | Establish the `env` interpreter's native loading prerequisites and resolve actual `node` under the same native context, without spawning either. Retain that selection for the later Node observation. Arbitrary env option languages are not added. A missing/unprovable chain prevents the DSH probe; no version probe may discover the obstruction first. |
| macOS native image | Apply the same evidence boundary using a bounded Mach-O header/load-command reader: target slice for a universal image, valid command ranges and executable type, and the `LC_LOAD_DYLINKER` prerequisite. Inspect the target loader rather than accepting a Mach-O magic value. Unsupported or unprovable slices/loader forms refuse explicitly. Linux's shebang limit and ELF rules do not apply here. |
| Windows native image | Native path selection is resolved before creation, so it must not inherit Unix execvp continuation. Admit ordinary native sentinel/Node images with valid bounded PE headers, machine/subsystem and image ranges plus the OS binary-type check. A malformed image, dangling target or unprovable launch prerequisite refuses at that candidate. Batch/script dispatch is a distinct interpreter form, never PE by extension; unsupported dispatch refuses before probing. No DLL loading or trial process is used as inspection. |

This reads loading declarations; it does not relocate, execute, resolve symbols,
emulate arbitrary binfmt handlers or walk shared-library dependencies as a
substitute loader. The identity being established is the selected executable,
not its eventual application exit status. For example, shared-library failure
after a successful exec cannot make execvp select B. Where a target's native
selection still depends on an unproved loading operation, take the named D10
refusal. No inference from a readable prefix supplies missing evidence.
Linux opens `PT_INTERP` during exec and checks its image; this is why the
candidate and its interpreter both need inspection. See the
[Linux ELF loader](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/torvalds/linux/master/fs/binfmt_elf.c)
and [script loader](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/torvalds/linux/master/fs/binfmt_script.c).
Mach-O's analogous prerequisite is documented by the
[XNU loader](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/apple-oss-distributions/xnu/main/bsd/kern/mach_loader.c).
Windows' [binary-type query](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/winbase/nf-winbase-getbinarytypew)
is format evidence, not a proof of arbitrary loader success.

Ordinary native sentinel, shell, `env` and Node positives are required on the
claimed targets. A blanket native-image refusal does not implement this table.
If the chosen inspection cannot establish one of those required positives,
report the unresolved implementation proof instead of weakening acceptance or
silently broadening admission. This decision chooses the evidence boundary;
it does not declare that an unimplemented recognizer already satisfies it.
Same-host concurrent mutation and transient spawn resource failures remain
observation limits, not excuses for deterministic fixture mismatches.

##### Scenario: Native default success and home failure remain independent

- **GIVEN** PATH absent, an ordinary proven `sh` on native default search and
  a same-named cwd decoy, with no DSH home
- **WHEN** native Command and DSH override selection observe identical context
- **THEN** selection identifies the native default executable, never the decoy
- **AND** the independent home refusal does not suppress a successful version
  probe; a failed version command keeps its own availability result
- **AND** a default-search miss instead has no target or probes, while explicit
  empty-entry lookup selects cwd only where the native rule does.

##### Scenario: Loading obstruction is refused before a version can hide it

- **GIVEN** A:B with runnable B and A containing a missing interpreter, an
  interpreter with a missing loader, or an ELF image whose `PT_INTERP` target
  does not exist
- **WHEN** the isolated native oracle identifies B and doctor sees the same layout
- **THEN** doctor names A and its precise prerequisite obstruction without
  executing A, its interpreter or B
- **AND** that refusal is recorded as the D10 exception, not native NotFound or
  a successful equality cell; ELOOP retains its separate terminal cause
  on Linux only — glibc stops there (measured 2026-09-20), Apple's search
  continues (Apple libc exec.c, posix_spawn.c); the cell is platform-qualified
  and asserted against the running platform's native control, per the
  2026-09-20 spec-defect correction of AS1.

**3. Structural parsing before scalar admission (findings 2 and 3).**

Keep `pnpm_dependencies`, `pnpm_flow_map`, `pnpm_scalar` and `Scalar::Plain /
Quoted`. One private mapping-separation helper recognizes admitted ASCII
indent/key/colon/padding boundaries and returns the unchanged scalar slice
with field context. Empty block mappings and inline values have different
separation requirements; a header or inline value requires the admitted space
after its colon. Audit all callers, including blank-line detection, top-level
sections, package headings, outer resolution and inner fields. Unicode trim
is not a structural operation. Only grammar-assigned ASCII padding outside
the scalar may be consumed; quoted interiors are never padding.

Recognize whole quote-aware flow tokens before decoding. Plain colon-space
and flow collection syntax refuse in every field, including ignored tarball.
Keep supported colon-without-space URLs, quoted punctuation and the separate
plain/quoted version-9 rule. Check field-sensitive scalar kind and then raw
identity data; only admitted values reach deduplication/serialization. Do not
reject all numeric ignored fields or introduce dependency semantics there.

| Input through the sole producer | Required responsible refusal |
|---|---|
| `lockfileVersion:9.0` | pnpm header lacks admitted colon separation |
| `resolution:{integrity: sha512-X}` | pnpm outer resolution lacks separation |
| `resolution: {integrity: sha512-X, tarball: x: y}` | pnpm `tarball` carries unsupported plain flow syntax |
| Plain integrity with U+00A0 at either edge, or either quoted counterpart | pnpm package/key context and `integrity carries whitespace` |

Unicode blank lines/key padding cannot disappear before these checks. Preserve
nonempty/NUL/all-whitespace identity checks, typed-plain refusal, decoded-key
uniqueness before exclusions, complete-triple deduplication, inclusive 8 MiB
reading and the sole serializer. No formatter, YAML dependency or extra
line/entry limit follows. Valid measured input keeps its pinned composite.

##### Scenario: Plain identity bytes cannot become structural padding

- **GIVEN** a valid separated lock and variants adding U+00A0 to each edge of
  plain, single-quoted and double-quoted integrity
- **WHEN** the real producer reads each complete lock
- **THEN** all variants refuse with `integrity carries whitespace`, while the
  unchanged control remains readable with its existing composite
- **AND** each malformed separator/colon-space vector refuses for its own
  syntax cause before scalar normalization can repair it.

**4. Independent executable/home assertions (finding 5).**

Correct `dsh_seams_resolve_reads_the_home_and_refuses_a_missing_one` with four
controlled combinations. Successful combined resolution requires both selected
executable and home. Missing executable wins as selection refusal regardless
of home; selected executable/missing home retains the path and home cause.
Use `selected_from`/`resolved` for deterministic units and the existing doctor
probe seam for exact zero/one probe counts. Remove stale comments promising
a returned declaration on selection failure.

The real child control sets HOME=/tmp and PATH=/usr/bin:/bin and removes
BROKKR_DSH_BIN, FORGE_DSH_BIN and DSH_HOME. Record native lookup there, not an
assumption about the developer's install. When it misses, a home still cannot
make resolution succeed; when an installed DSH exists, use its actual result
and retain the separate deterministic missing-executable case. The commissioned
failing environment must be reproduced on a suitable host before closure; an
unavailable prerequisite remains pending rather than a passing skip. No parent
process environment mutation or provider installation is needed.

**5. Native matrix and removal evidence.**

Name the shared table `native_executable_resolution_matches_command_matrix`.
Cross **all eight** rows (`dsh`, `./dsh`, `../dsh`, an absolute temporary
`.../dsh`, literal `C:\Tools\dsh.exe`, `dsh.exe`, a name with a space and a
NUL-bearing name) with all layouts: cwd-only with PATH elsewhere; a PATH-dir
candidate plus a competing cwd file; absent PATH; present-empty and leading/
interior/trailing empty entries; A:B with each of the three loader obstructions.
Direct-path rows still cross all layouts. Add ELOOP, ordinary non-executable
A/runnable B, valid native A, override precedence and no-fallback controls.
For the Linux missing-loader cell, patch only the interpreter-path bytes in
an owned copy of a working native sentinel, preserving valid ELF layout; assert
that the native child really runs B. Use that same obstructed image as a
script's interpreter for the chain cell. A generic malformed executable is
not evidence of the commissioned missing-loader continuation.

Run resolver and native oracle within an isolated child already possessing the
fixture cwd and environment. On Windows match parent PATH, executable directory
and explicit-child-PATH state too; copying a helper/doctor into the same owned
application directory makes competing application/PATH candidates testable.
Use native Rust sentinels reporting their own executable identity; script cells
use unique markers mapped to candidate paths. Normalized canonical file identity
is compared, not spelling, equal versions or a success boolean. Give native
oracle and doctor separate marker destinations so B's oracle execution cannot
pollute the no-probe assertion. Built-doctor negatives retain the shipped
unmeasured declaration and assert marker/sentinel absence before reason text.

Every ordinary success asserts exact selected identity; native NotFound asserts
no target plus the cause. NUL compares native InvalidInput with an up-front
NUL refusal and zero resolver I/O/probes. Terminal failures preserve cause.
Obstructed cells report native outcome separately and assert candidate/cause
and zero production probes. The same matrix runs natively on Windows with
owned executable fixtures. Keep the literal drive spelling as a native path
case there; never create an unauthorized `C:\Tools` installation or rename it
into an ordinary cwd filename. An unavailable native fixture is pending proof,
not a successful skip. Unix loader encodings cannot establish Windows results;
the Windows obstruction control uses native broken-image/launch prerequisites
and records whether its native result is terminal rather than assuming B.

AO's additional `absent_path_default_search_matches_native_dsh_and_node`
requires default-search `sh` through primary and legacy DSH overrides, actual
`node` lookup with native-positive identity (test-only `process.execPath` is
suitable), cwd decoys, present-empty controls with and without a cwd candidate,
and explicit-PATH success. A shell result cannot substitute for Node; absent
native-positive evidence remains pending. No installed system location changes.

| Repair | Named test and compiling removal that must fail meaningfully |
|---|---|
| S1 | `absent_path_refuses_before_doctor_can_execute_a_cwd_sentinel`: turn absent PATH into an empty entry; fail first on sentinel execution, then restore and assert native default-search miss. |
| S1b/platform rule | `unix_backslash_names_follow_native_lookup_before_doctor_probe` plus the full matrix: restore backslash-as-separator in each enforcing path independently; fail on forbidden execution/selected identity under absent and populated PATH. |
| Native default success | `absent_path_default_search_matches_native_dsh_and_node`: restore unconditional absent-PATH refusal; native positive still runs and selected-identity assertion fails. The withdrawn inverse test is not required. |
| Loader obstruction | `an_obstructed_path_search_takes_the_explicit_safe_refusal`: independently restore unconditional non-shebang admission and one-level interpreter metadata admission; dynamic-loader and interpreter-chain cells fail the named obstruction/no-probe assertions. |
| Terminal versus continuation | `the_candidate_classifier_stops_where_the_child_stops_and_refuses_the_unprovable`: erase terminal-error distinction; real-child comparison detects forbidden continuation. |
| Pnpm syntax | `missing_pnpm_field_separation_and_unsupported_flow_syntax_refuse_by_reason` through `composite_over_pnpm`: independently remove header separation, outer separation and ignored-field colon-space checks; each intended cause assertion fails. |
| Identity bytes | `pnpm_integrity_preserves_unicode_whitespace_for_refusal` (chosen council name): restore Unicode trimming before scalar validation; plain edge-NBSP values become wrongly readable while quoted/valid controls discriminate. |
| Home/executable premise | Corrected `dsh_seams_resolve_reads_the_home_and_refuses_a_missing_one`: restore the home-only test assumption in the controlled missing-executable child and reproduce its failure. This is a test-premise mutation, not an invented production repair. |

For each mutation record changed rule, exact test command, nonzero selected test
count, intended failed assertion, exact restoration and green rerun. Compilation
failure, unrelated failure, a removed fixture, bare `is_err()` or zero-test
filter proves nothing. Mutations run only in implementation and are fully
restored before final gates. Existing producer/removal regressions remain.

**Dependency handoff within the declared artifact order.** Proposal AO and AS1
already encode the answered ambiguity, native matrix and scalar scenarios;
neither requires amendment. This phase revises their dependent design only.
The next tasks phase updates the existing breakdown before implementation:

| 8.8.2.1–8.8.2.2 | Bounded candidate/interpreter evidence, three loader obstructions, full matrix and named refusal/terminal distinction. |
| 8.8.3.1 | Complete raw header/outer/inner grammar audit, three independent syntax removals and Unicode-byte preservation test/removal. |
| 8.8.8.1–8.8.8.4 | Correct the F5 premise with four cases and real environment wiring; consolidate new evidence; all seven green crates plus workspace tests; unchanged fresh exact gate and actual integers; no inherited roster exception. |

These amendments reuse clause addresses, keep global 8.8 unchecked and do not
re-author completed decoded-key/sole-producer/diagnostic repairs. Historical
ticks do not certify newly strengthened clauses. AO and this sitting supersede
contradictory current task wording pending that owning revision; they are not
competing requirements. No new task IDs, decision status changes, frozen-file
edits or gate instructions in notes follow. Delivery still owes all five repairs,
removal executions, green gates and native-platform proof. Excluded work remains
part (d), 8.10, 9.6, 10.6–10.8, 11.1–11.4 and groups 14–15.

#### Prior retained-input DSH composite sitting — 2026-09-19, adopted `9ccfc68d`

The following is the retained sitting at its own source baseline. The current
sitting above supersedes its selection/probe interpretation and implementation
status; its measured-input invariants and composite suffix table remain adopted.

This sitting adopts AK, AS1 and proposed 0056 without reopening their decisions.
Both current positions were read completely through workspace hands:

- robustness: `1af6d71b30151936ae32c1b34421b96bc0a6fbccde21b54b002a2e78840ae4da`
- simplicity: `bc48aaca2f767a031a2a84956adbdabab778ef0a022bbe96b9008334cfacd95e`

Their current claims agree on the needed mechanisms. Neither now proposes the
single-read cut or a digest newtype discussed by the predecessor council. The
table distinguishes that agreement from independent source evidence and states
which alternatives are refused. No upstream artifact is at fault: AK and AS1
already withdrew the synthetic-fixture narrowing; this design is its owner here.

| Current position / claim | Disposition, evidence and alternative |
|---|---|
| Both: adopt the committed work and complete only (a), then (b), then (c). | Adopt. Keep the existing modules, D6 byte form and proposed 0056. Add no task ID, contract, stored field, dependency, subcommand or support declaration. Part (d) receives no new plan or production change. |

A serialization-only assertion cannot prove that selection reaches `Start.input.resume_context.assessment`; extend the existing runtime start-path tests with the exact selected value and omission. Keep it distinct from `originating_wrapper_digest`. Refusals must name field and grammar/type reason. |
| Robustness §2 / simplicity (b): seal one producer and retain sources once. | Combine their narrow ownership changes. `adapters.rs` exports intermediate producers; `resolve_core` reopens the selected manifest, the outer producer rereads its hidden lock, and `plugin-patch` rehashes an already observed file. Retain those values within one observation. Expose only the entry point, seams, read access and necessary error surface; synthetic construction stays test-only. Reject the monolith, generic reader framework, digest newtype and atomic snapshot service: none is needed to prevent these concrete contradictions. |
| Robustness §3 / simplicity (b): exact membership and metadata before the direct dependency-directory exception. | Adopt. `walk` currently skips the name before inspecting its type, descends into arbitrary directories and normalizes names lossily. D6 now admits only the fixed paths, necessary ancestors and a real direct `node_modules/` directory. Reject empty/deeper/unsafe/symlink/special entries with their safely rendered path and reason. Reuse the private computation for the existing conditional extension; no inventory API or new extension is needed. |

Exclude only the exact core hidden-lock key and applicable local-tarball records; validate all npm groups and take the terminal spelling with that entry's fields. The retained lock distinguishes 521 records, 501 triples and 489 names, so a name map, slash-only split or count-only proof is demonstrably insufficient. |
| Robustness §5 / simplicity (b): bounded fail-closed pnpm grammar and complete scalar validation. | Adopt the existing parser approach with a bounded raw read of at most 8,388,609 bytes; 8,388,608 is inclusive. The measured 1,982 bytes support the selected budget. Validate structural context, quoting, permitted children and resolution rather than skipping unknown lines. Validate source scalars before their intentional triple separators; do not trim Node output or strip a forbidden CR from the shebang. Reject YAML dependencies and extra npm/line/count caps because they add uncommissioned semantics. All 8.10 vectors remain pending. |
| Robustness §6 / simplicity (b): preserve lookup order and canonical containment. | Adopt the existing `Profile { dir, canonical }` separation and verify it. Canonicalize the boundary once, compare components, and refuse canonicalization failure/outside first hit. Reject canonicalizing the lookup anchor, raw/string-prefix fallback and later-hit substitution: each changes the selected installation. Source already corrects the historical symlinked-home defect; no reimplementation credit is due. |
| Robustness §6: missing versus unreadable sources and first executable semantics; simplicity: keep the existing locator/error seams. | Combine. `home_patch::exists()` can misreport a dangling symlink as `absent`; `resolve_bundle::is_file()` can skip failed candidate metadata, and PATH lookup admits ordinary non-executable files. Preserve actual absence separately from failure, follow executable child-PATH selection and return the responsible component with safe locator/reason. Do not add provider discovery, fallback locks or a public error taxonomy. |
| Both fixture sections / robustness §8: literal complete measured inputs, both anchors and full dependency equality. | Adopt AK in D6 and Context, replacing the predecessor's narrowing. The supplied hashes match the complete raw bytes and six committed files. Embed them in the existing Rust test source; compare the complete measured ordered expectations and all components. Reject `.forge/` runtime/build access, derived lists as lock inputs, compression/generation and synthetic substitutes. Expected plugin/composite digests come only from the sole producer, then are pinned and mutation-proved; remove competing test serializers. |
| Robustness §7 / simplicity (c): one DSH seam for version and composite, bounded to this provider. | Adopt. The controller's two sentinel controls agree with `probe_providers` probing `adapter.binary` before `dsh_composite_line` resolves again. Resolve once, version-probe the selected executable, and pass those seams to the producer. Prove primary, legacy and PATH precedence and selected failure without fallback. Reject provider-wide unification despite the similar defect elsewhere: it expands the commissioned behavior. |

An unreadable value has no equality result, so neither a match nor a mismatch is truthful. Preserve the component/reason and declaration presence, apply the existing supported-and-declared warning predicate, and retain independent version availability. No new status or wire field follows. |

Use producer-derived measured-fixture output and the same rendered wording, explicitly attributed; no relabeling of the synthetic rc.1 sample and no claim of the retained-home recording. Only DSH/Node version probes belong to this identity diagnostic; no credentials/settings, resolver subprocess, model call, configuration dump or live-home mutation. |
| Both proof sections: every substantive assertion needs a compiling removal and exact reason. | Adopt the bindings below. The existing missing-file test leaves an extra file present, and the near-prefix test can stop at the base bundle before its purported plugin assertion. Isolate each cause and assert its responsible path/reason. Compilation failure, an earlier unrelated assertion, self-equality and coverage percentages are not removal evidence. |
| Both cut/risk sections: no broader subsystem, migration, enablement or qualification. | Adopt all cuts with their costs acknowledged. Accept the large inline literal, strict future-syntax refusal, dependency-lock rather than full-tree identity, and later same-host mutation. Keep the declaration unmeasured/disabled, 0056 proposed, extension bytes frozen and non-DSH doctor behavior unchanged. Rust gates, exact coverage and real removal records remain implementation obligations. |

**Doctor state decision.** The following classifies the composite suffix only;
ordinary failed version probes retain their existing availability warning. A
home-resolution error is a named composite/seam failure and must not erase a
successfully observed version or fabricate a missing-binary result. Preserve
the adapter's binary selection even when the remaining home seam fails, without
performing a second environment resolution or trying another installation.

| Composite result | Declared digest | Shape | Composite suffix and severity |
|---|---|---|---|
| Readable | Absent | Any | Canonical/plugin values; `no declared wrapper_digest`; informational. |
| Readable, equal | Present | Any | Canonical/plugin values; `matches the declared wrapper_digest`; informational. |
| Readable, different | Present | `supported` | Values; `differs from the declared wrapper_digest <digest>`; warning. |
| Readable, different | Present | Other | Same comparison; informational. |
| Unreadable | Absent | Any | `composite unreadable: <component/reason>` and `no declared wrapper_digest`; informational. |
| Unreadable | Present | `supported` | Same reason and `declared wrapper_digest <digest>; comparison unavailable`; warning. |
| Unreadable | Present | Other | Same declaration/unavailable wording; informational. |

##### Scenario: Unreadable does not invent an equality result

- **GIVEN** the selected DSH executable returns a version and the composite
  producer refuses the pnpm lock with its component and specific reason
- **WHEN** doctor renders the DSH line with or without a declared digest
- **THEN** that version remains visible beside the sanitized unreadable reason
- **AND** no declaration yields `no declared wrapper_digest`; a declaration
  yields its digest and `comparison unavailable`, never `matches` or `differs`
- **AND** the composite warns only for a `supported` shape with that declaration;
  unrelated binary-availability warnings retain their own meaning.

##### Scenario: Literal retained bytes exercise both real bundle anchors

- **GIVEN** the complete measured input constants and six unchanged plugin files
  materialized at the core/profile locators in D6, without a runtime `.forge/` read
- **WHEN** the sole producer resolves the declared three bundles and reads both locks
- **THEN** both built-ins resolve under core and the plugin under profile, and the
  complete ordered 502 dependency values and all measured component fields match
- **AND** collapsing by name, omitting a differing triple, moving a built-in to
  the profile, taking an ancestor npm name or including an excluded record fails
  its own exact assertion, even if a total count happens to remain unchanged
- **AND** producer-derived pinned digests are fixture results; they make no claim
  that the later retained-home recording or task 8.8 acceptance is complete.

##### Scenario: A present unreadable home patch is not absence

- **GIVEN** otherwise readable fixed locators and a home patch represented by a
  dangling symlink or a known-present entry whose metadata/bytes cannot be read
- **WHEN** the producer observes `home-patch`
- **THEN** it refuses with `home-patch`, a safe locator and the specific reason
- **AND** a separate truly missing-path control produces the literal `absent`;
  a successful digest cannot be manufactured from the failed observation.

Remove serialization or one grammar/key guard; the owning assertion must fail. An originating checkpoint digest is not the declaration assertion. |

Remove an exclusion, collapse by name, corrupt terminal-name selection or a serialized line/order; name the exact value/digest assertion that fails. |

For every actual control record the production mutation, exact test, exact
failed assertion, restoration and passing rerun in the existing delivery
account. Do not prefill outcomes from this table. Platform-specific cases keep
their native guards; capable host/CI results must exist before any coverage
claim. The literal 100% line/branch/function gate is never relaxed.

Source confirms `load.rs` admits the optional member only on `Measured`, validates 64 lowercase hex, and serializes it through the selected assessment. Implementation verifies and mutation-proves those lines; it adds no type, field or second validator. |
| Both: “one Rust function” means one callable production producer with private helpers, not a literal monolith. Simplicity asks for a visibility trim in the existing module; robustness asks that callers cannot supply or obtain intermediate digest values. | Combine. Keep `composite.rs`, expose only the seams, structured observation, error and `dsh_composite` at the smallest required production scope, and make serializers, file digests, lock normalizers and injected helpers private or test-only. No file, module, crate, subcommand or public capability is added. |
| Robustness: retain each identity-bearing source once so one returned observation cannot contradict itself. Simplicity: cut this invariant because later same-host mutation is already a recorded residual and the extra plumbing has coverage cost. | Adopt robustness on the two evidenced reopenings and reject the cut. `resolve_core` reads the hidden lock before `dsh_composite_with` reads it again, while `cordis.patch.yml` is hashed inside the plugin component and reopened for `plugin-patch`. A mutation between either pair can make the returned component fields and canonical digest describe no single observation. D6's residual accepts mutation after an observation/check; it does not justify self-contradiction created by reopening a source inside one call. Retain the hidden-lock bytes and plugin file digests once and reuse them. This is not an atomic snapshot, watcher or continuous verifier. |
| Both: plugin membership needs direct-only `node_modules/`, exact path bytes and named drift. Robustness also calls out empty directories, metadata-before-skip, symlinks and special files; simplicity identifies the lossy conversion and skip-at-any-depth defects. | Adopt the union because the commission admits exactly six files plus the dependency subtree. Permit only a direct, real, non-symlink `<plugin>/node_modules/` directory. Any other file or directory, including an empty one or a deeper `node_modules`, is drift. Refuse non-UTF-8 names, symlinks and special entries with the safe relative name or unrepresentable-name reason; never use `to_string_lossy`. |
| Both: the pnpm read must be bounded before allocation and its grammar closed. Simplicity limits this cap to the pnpm lock; robustness specifies the fail-closed raw reader and grammar details. | Combine. The existing no-YAML state machine reads at most 8,388,609 raw bytes for `pnpm-lock.yaml`, admits exactly 8,388,608 to grammar validation, and refuses the next byte with the fixed reason. It admits only the measured lockfile-9 vocabulary and reason-refuses unknown structure. The hidden npm lock has its fixed locator and complete grammar but gains no uncommissioned size cap. |
| Both: complete whitespace rejection is still missing and must apply to every source scalar without trimming. | Adopt the uniform reading fixed by amended AS1: names, versions, integrities and every source scalar entering a line reject empty/NUL/all whitespace. Space, tab, CR and LF are explicit vectors. `node --version` may have its one record terminator, but the extracted line is not repaired with `trim()`. Tests assert the responsible field/reason, never bare `is_err()`. |
| Both: exclusions must identify exact lock records rather than package names. | Adopt. The inherited `excluded: &[&str]` drops every matching name, which can erase a distinct registry version or integrity. Exclude the core's exact own hidden-lock record and only the plugin or conditional-extension local-tarball records whose installed bytes enter component lines. Retain same-named registry triples, deduplicate equal complete triples only, then sort value bytes. |
| Both: errors must name the responsible component. Robustness asks for structured component, safe locator and reason; simplicity prefers extending the existing variants rather than a new public taxonomy. | Combine. Use the smallest internal representation that preserves stable component, safe locator/reason and drifted path. An unreadable profile or home patch cannot be reported as a plugin-component failure. No new public error hierarchy is required. |
| Robustness: carry generated hashes in an internal 64-lowercase-hex type. Simplicity: cut the type because SHA-256 encoding is lowercase by construction and the loader already validates the external declaration. | Reject the newtype. After the production helpers are sealed, generated digests originate only at `hex::encode(Sha256::digest(...))`, and the producer accepts no caller-supplied intermediate hash. The declaration remains independently checked at load. A new internal type would add branches and plumbing without a reachable malformed-generated-digest refusal. |

Expected digests still originate only from the sole Rust producer. |
| Both: doctor's composite already resolves `DshSeams`, but its version still probes `adapter.binary` on PATH. Robustness asks for one resolved installation; simplicity asks for the smallest DSH-only correction. | Combine. Resolve DSH seams once inside the existing DSH branch, probe that executable, and pass the same seams to the producer. Do not add a provider-wide registry or change non-DSH probes. Keep every declared-digest disposition and warning rule, make unreadable details terminal-safe, and update the guide sample from the same vocabulary. |
| Both: every substantive claim needs a compiling remove/fail/restore/pass control and every refusal assertion must name its reason. Literal 100% line/branch/function coverage remains required for added lines. The complete 8.10 vector ledger stays pending even where (b) necessarily lands shared behavior. |
| Both: no speculative extension, parser crate, generic seam registry, second producer, subcommand, cache/watcher, evidence store, contract/store/wire field, task ID, support declaration or planner work. | Adopt every cut. Keep the conditional extension branch synthetic because the measured profile does not name it. Decision 0056 stays `proposed`. |

A panicking composite closure proves no recomputation but a nonexistent executable does not prove that a version probe was never attempted; use a recording version shim and assert zero calls. | Robustness: qualification is an equality chain over observed, declared and originating identity; absence never removes a constraint. An origin mismatch necessarily follows the current observations, so reject a blanket assertion that every declined offer calls no producer. The zero-call assertion belongs to disabled/pre-observation failures. |
| Both: require the offered provider ID and enforce the locator bound. Simplicity: a byte-length input check is sufficient; robustness: the producer and consumer must agree. | Combine the local guards with robustness's complete round-trip invariant. `Transcript::record` clamps by characters, so reject a byte-count substitute and a consumer-only check. No new locator type or transcript contract is needed. |
| Simplicity: canonicalize the selected file inside the root; no blanket prohibition on every symlink. Robustness: the root and locator must denote the originating store. | Adopt both checks. Reject robustness's alternative of declaring ID/header agreement sufficient: that would weaken the already-specified originating address. Keep canonical aliases within the same root admissible and reject selected-file escape into another root, even inside the same home. |
| Robustness: `dsh_session_header_id` and `dsh_session_last_seq` allocate without bounds before spawn. Simplicity: the walk is bounded by its exact-one rule and sequence work belongs to D. | Adopt bounded admission IO; reject those reasons to leave it unbounded. Exact-one selection bounds the result cardinality, not directory enumeration or file allocation. D6 bounds these existing pre-spawn reads and declines incomplete evidence; it does not implement C's child exchange or D's fold/dedup/usage semantics. |
| Robustness: cover joined model, duplicate/invalid effort and unknown residual controls; derive rejection from the declaration. Simplicity: add only duplicate effort and do not guess aliases or add a generic grammar. | Combine the demonstrated closures and DSH-local scope; reject deriving the whole CLI grammar from the two declaration flag names and reject a growing deny-list as sufficient. The committed plugin startup and task-owned launcher expose the actual control surfaces, including `--from-default-profile`; D6 permits the existing extracted input forms and no unverified residual. No invented `-m`/`-e` support, generic parser or shared Codex/Claude splitter change follows. |
| Robustness: refusals must not echo values. Simplicity: the existing `Err(String)` and bounded vocabulary suffice without a new error type. | Adopt both. The representation can stay; the content must change. `dsh_control_conflict` returns the whole token, `split_dsh_patch` formats an odd token, and `parse_dsh_model` formats the rejected model. D6 requires fixed field/category diagnostics across those paths, proven with synthetic private markers absent from errors. No new refusal token or diagnostic field is needed. |
| Both: reader tests and direct overlay folding do not prove the cold/offered/disabled planner matrix. Simplicity: keep the grammar suite, avoid a second case catalogue or redundant conformance test. | Adopt. 8.10 remains the case catalogue. Extend its existing planner suite with the required cross-path outcomes and pre-read/pre-stage ordering; reuse reader vectors and prove digest failure precedes shape validation with a file invalid on both axes. Retain Pass A's separate engine tests. A built-driver test is justified only by a distinct planner observation; no child-confirmation case is added in B. |
| Simplicity's cuts: no module, new plan/root type, public seam, digest/manifest producer, dependency, schema mirror, wire/store/contract change, scenario or task ID, live Cargo test, provider proof, recipe/witness/pair edit or task tick. Robustness: no enablement or C/D work. | Adopt these cuts. The private home metadata completes an existing requirement and is not a public wire/record field. Reject the proposed roster-comment edit as already delivered in `1d21319`. Keep 0056 proposed, DSH unmeasured, all provider minima and LaneTally's sole 11.4 exception. | Both: retain all fail-closed checks, identity dates, pending enablement, frozen surfaces and task ticks. | Adopt. No C/D, live qualification, new grammar, new coverage exclusion, archive, living-spec fold, global installation or remote action. A digest test detects drift; documentation alone states never-publish and the test cannot prevent publication. |

**4. Composition-to-launch proof and controls.** Two connected assertions are
required, under existing AS1/AS2/LE5; no new production seam or duplicate engine
topology suite is authorized.

1. In the existing runtime boundary/agent test suites, load the real Codex
   declaration through `Adapters::load` and the production candidate resolver
   for a work agent with hands. Under `Boundary::Harness`, compose the actual
   candidate and `SiteSpawn`, and read the work input from `seat_input`/
   `mark_hands`. Assert `boundary: harness`, absent `hands`, the shipped
   `--sandbox workspace-write` fragment, model/effort and no workspace MCP
   fragment. Assert that the declaration's scalar scope matches these facts.
   Pair it with boxed resolution: full `hands.workspace` including MCP `-c`,
   boxed input facts, and no claim of supported rejoin. These are composition
   assertions, so no nested namespace needs to be spawned for the bridge.
2. In `crates/brokkr-cli/tests/driver_conformance.rs`, use that same shipped
   assessment and the production-resolved/composed harness argv (existing
   resolver and `compose_site` APIs), with the exact harness input pinned by
   the runtime assertion. No hand-written shortened workspace command. In
   test-owned homes a deterministic shim answers `--version` with
   `codex-cli 0.153.4`, records argv and announces a root before current work.
   A cold invocation establishes the root; a fresh driver process receives
   `hello`, a correctly correlated `resume` for it, `start`, `shutdown`.
   Assert actual `exec resume`, exactly that thread, re-expressed
   `sandbox_mode="workspace-write"`, effort, current prompt/result destination,
   and the production adapter's confirmed `launch: resumed`. The shim emits
   provider events, never fabricated launch rows. Keep engine eligibility/
   ownership tests beside this provider proof as required below.

##### Scenario: The engine-composed harness work seat keeps main's rejoin

- **GIVEN** the shipped Codex adapter resolved for a work seat with hands under
  harness, producing `boundary: harness`, no `hands` marker and
  `--sandbox workspace-write`, and an eligible owned thread at 0.153.4
- **WHEN** a retry crosses the production driver with that assessment and argv
- **THEN** it rejoins that exact thread with current sandbox/effort re-expressed
  and provider-confirmed `launch: resumed`, despite historical evidence 0.148.0
- **AND** reverting shipped status to `unmeasured` fails this behavioral proof
  with `unsupported-resume`; reverting only declared hands to `boxed` fails
  it with `restrictions-unavailable`, reproducing analyze F1
- **AND** a boxed work seat with its full MCP-bearing argv stays cold; neither
  passing a synthetic read-only command nor reading status proves preservation

First run the positive against the adopted disabled declaration and retain its
runtime failure. After the declared correction passes, independently restore
shipped `status: unmeasured` and then `hands: boxed`; rebuild and observe the
specified launch assertion fail for each, restoring exact bytes and passing
between controls. Removing accounting is an additional refusal control, never
a substitute for either status or F1's hands control. For the composition
bridge, temporarily suppress harness fragment append in `compose_site`, and
separately suppress the boundary write in `mark_hands`; the corresponding
assertions must fail, restore/pass. Test failures must identify the claimed
behavior, not a compile error or unrelated assertion.

The negative boxed case uses the complete resolved workspace fragment with its
MCP arguments and requires cold `restrictions-unavailable` under the narrowed
scope, without a version probe. Preserve the existing allow-list refusal for
MCP `-c` independently; no attempt to authorize those arguments is in scope.
Other negatives cover missing/unreadable observed identity, observed historical
or third-version identity, recorded origin mismatch, boundary/hands mismatch,
missing accounting and different confirmed root. All three other shipped
assessments refuse otherwise eligible offers as `unsupported-resume` without
probing. Existing `engine/resume_tests.rs` retains site/instance/local-origin/
manifest/latest-owner, legacy-offer and fresh-gate coverage. Preserve its narrow
legacy mapping; do not manufacture missing historical identity fields.

Every new behavioral assertion owes a compiling break/observed failure/exact
restore/passing rerun on the predicate it protects. The bridge and driver
proof are deterministic evidence of composition and launch, not installed
provider enforcement. Record commands, mutations and observed failures in the
existing task account. No claim of full 10.5/11.1 qualification follows.

**5. Four lows, in commissioned order.**

1. **DSH cold sequence zero:** retain `Option<u64>` from the cold/warm plan
   through `drain_dsh_transcript` to `fold_dsh_event`. Cold is `None`; warm is
   `Some(last_stored_seq)`, including zero. Keep both
   `a_cold_dsh_launch_folds_its_first_event_and_a_warm_one_folds_past_the_boundary`
   and `the_planned_dsh_fold_boundary_reaches_the_transcript_drain`. Before any
   further fix, temporarily restore `None`-as-zero filtering and observe the
   cold-zero test fail; restore/pass. Separately restore the cold plan's
   `Some(0)` seed and observe the actual plan-to-drain test fail; restore/pass.
   Retain warm exclusion at/below zero, no-sequence rows and both qualified and
   shipped-disabled cold routes. The plugin's inclusive first-current sequence
   and Rust's exclusive last-stored boundary are distinct and unchanged.
2. **Codex refusal cause:** retain selector refusal first, then on an offer
   gate decline before ID, sandbox and compatible-resume-argv checks, with no
   probe for a disabled gate. Move the gate block below the sandbox split as a
   temporary control; `a_closed_gate_names_its_own_reason_ahead_of_the_seat_s_local_checks`
   must observe `sandbox-unavailable` where it expects `unsupported-resume`.
   Restore/pass. Preserve unchanged safe cold argv, enabled local-defect checks,
   and no-offer/no-refusal behavior. An enabled no-offer cold plan may still
   probe identity to record a useful root; no blanket no-probe rule is added.
3. **Codex cold selector:** retain the existing conservative `resume` word
   refusal even in value positions. Temporarily bypass its guard and observe
   `a_codex_seat_argv_that_selects_a_session_is_refused_on_the_cold_path_too`
   fail; restore/pass. Cover no offer, offered and disabled-shape paths before
   any provider work, with no handle echoed. Add only the already-planned
   replacement-call comment: `invoke` reaches direct `codex_cold` only after
   successful `codex_launch` with the same immutable arguments. No duplicate
   builder guard, silent argument stripping or provider exception.
4. **DSH never-publish:** adopt `extensions/dsh/PROVENANCE.md`'s explicit
   prohibition and retain all six upstream-attributed files unchanged. Extend
   `the_committed_plugin_set_is_the_six_files_and_the_one_expression_delta`
   to compare the full returned digest map with the six committed-adapted
   digests recorded in PROVENANCE; retain the one-expression reversal check.
   Calibrate the comparison with an isolated copy containing a one-byte
   manifest change, temporarily point the test there, observe failure, then
   restore the test and pass on committed bytes. Verify all six bytes directly
   against PROVENANCE too. This detects drift, not publication; no manifest
   `private: true`, registry probe, publisher or new plugin delta is authorized.

**6. Measurement and handoff.** Before and after implementation run the
unchanged exact gate with a writable disk-backed `TMPDIR` outside the repository;
never use the filling `/tmp` tmpfs or an in-repository directory for coverage.
Prefix commands with `env -u GIT_CONFIG_COUNT -u GIT_CONFIG_VALUE_0`, including
parent test commands so child Git calls inherit #282's correction. Record
command, exit status, revision/changed-byte identity, pinned compiler/tool
versions and JSON/LCOV/summary hashes beside the gate's three covered/total
pairs. The inherited **30,015/30,015 lines, 5,036/5,036 branches,
2,861/2,861 functions** is prior evidence, not a fresh before run. Preserve
`rust-nightly-version.txt` agreement across CI, release admission and coverage.
Do not repeat the settled reader-seam investigation or change the gate,
`coverage(off)`, conditional exclusions, thresholds or witness assertions.

Run affected suites, `cargo test --workspace`, locked all-feature workspace
tests, all-target/all-feature locked clippy with warnings denied, format, both
`bundles/self` and `bundles/verify` compiles, and strict active OpenSpec
validation. Report actual in-box coverage; #286 leaves host equality to the
controller. If this seat lacks the compiler or a suitable scratch mount, record
that limitation instead of inventing numbers or substituting the saved report.
The design commit changes no compiled source or declaration and makes no
runtime success claim. The task and implementation offices own the remaining
edits and measured controls; exact-head remote CI, integration and closure stay
pending their actual external results.

#### Whole-family council reconciliation — 2026-09-16, adopted `c2ed4f8`

Read both complete positions for `the-whole-family-branch-integrat-0a5cdb4f`:
robustness SHA-256
`27d1065f1d95d3d051e4caf9bc60fc47e3228e685e3f9263ef0357c390e0554c`,
simplicity SHA-256
`bfe196c892be9b3c6126863bfa658a9845380d7c5afb984a9fb33896e9b6b91b`.
Copies are retained under `.forge/design-whole-family-0a5cdb4f/`; shared position
paths can be reused. Both supply evidence, not routing authority. This replaces
the two-mediums sitting's defective mechanism details, preserving its dependency
order and legal collision constructions from `c56d091`/`4d7d452`. Proposal Y and
SR1/SR2/AS1/AS2/LE2/LE5 already own the required behavior; no upstream defect is
established and no new requirement or task is introduced.

**Evidence.** At `c2ed4f8`, `bundle.rs:1031–1035` collects assessment/witness
maps before the wrapper; `1308–1318` moves hands/records/drivers for the single
only. `mark_hands` (`engine.rs:1214–1221`) treats a missing hands entry as no
statement; `resume_gate` (`adapters.rs:942–946`) turns that absence into
permission. `refuse_aliasing_sites` (`bundle.rs:1538–1568`) compares each
selected body separately, while `structural_sites` (`resume.rs:712`) omits
`StepBody::Dialect`. Thus a model-only final walk cannot reserve validator
facts. `process.rs:115–128` directly executes argv[0]; it does not interpret a
text shebang on Windows. The predecessor review corroborates the consequences;
its patch is neither a template nor evidence that the fixes work.

**Chosen mechanism, in the adopted dependency order.**

1. **Register owners before collecting facts.** Walk the resolved authoring
   seats, including inline/agent leaves, every phase, selector case/default,
   sequence step/member and deterministic dialect leaf, before any assessment,
   effort witness, hands or agent/driver evidence can be inserted. Registration
   binds the full engine label to a structural owner: outer seat, selected case,
   body kind and ancestry, carried from the walk rather than split from `:`.
   Refuse a second distinct owner with `CompileError::Invalid`, naming the label
   and both owners, regardless of populated evidence. Repeated names at distinct
   addresses and same-owner evidence merges remain valid. This pre-collection
   guard is necessary even when wrapping would later separate the raw aliases.

2. **Make the family one value throughout compilation and execution.** Use one
   canonical `BTreeMap<String, SiteFacts>` with each entry bound to the census's
   structural owner and carrying the five fields: `inline_resume`, `pin_drivers`
   (effort/resume witnesses), `hands`, `record` (former `context.records`) and
   `driver` (former `context.drivers`). Collection and resolution populate the
   owning value, not independent authoritative maps that are zipped later.
   Same-owner driver/exemption/resume evidence uses the existing digest merge.
   Retain this table on `Bundle`; engine hands/assessment readers use this same
   source. Manifest `hands`/`agents`/`drivers` remain serialization projections
   with their existing vocabulary, not separately mutable runtime authorities.
   A sixth field follows automatically because relocation moves the value.

   Registration alone establishes an owner, not the absence of hands. The
   hands field must distinguish unresolved/unknown, positively resolved no-hands,
   and a resolved `HandsSpec` (a private enum is sufficient). Initialize unknown;
   resolve no-hands only after the actual inline or agent declaration has been
   read successfully and establishes no configured hands. Never default a missing
   entry or incomplete field to no-hands. This preserves the independent guard
   even if a future producer drops a field inside a registered site.

   At the wrapper, pair each parsed source owner with its preserved executing
   owner directly through that structural transformation: single `verify` becomes
   `verify:checks`; member `verify:<m>` becomes `verify:checks:<m>`. Carry the
   source owner and new ancestry together; do not zip separately filtered walks,
   scan prefixes, split labels or infer ancestry from iteration order. Drain all
   these source entries into a staged collection before inserting destinations.
   Move each complete `SiteFacts` once and update its ownership reservation
   in the same transformation; the census is compilation state, not a new
   persisted field or runtime authority.
   For `x` and `checks:x`, the first destination is the second source; draining
   first prevents an overwrite or false collision. A missing source stays unknown
   and cannot manufacture an empty, affirmatively no-hands replacement.

3. **Reserve final ownership at the transformation, then check it globally.**
   The table includes all authored owners before wrapping, even later-parsed and
   factless ones. After draining the moving owners, claim all destination labels
   and the injected validator's label before inserting relocated or synthetic
   facts; distinct owners refuse before overwrite. Synthetic and user-authored
   dialect steps reserve their own structural addresses without receiving model
   plans or offers. A complete final walk compares full engine labels across
   all phases/cases/defaults, including deterministic steps, using one global
   owner map and preserving within-body diagnostics. It checks structural
   ownership independently of which fields are populated. No ambiguous bundle
   is published. Raw reservations and transformation claims share the ownership
   rule; the final walk also checks that later compiler transformations did not
   introduce an alias. A transient overwrite discarded by a later error is not
   the chosen implementation.

4. **Publish only established confinement; refuse absence independently.** At
   each actual model dispatch, compose both markers from that executing owner's
   resolved hands state: no-hands gives `boundary: not applicable`, `hands: none`;
   hands gives the current boundary word and `boxed` only when the boundary is
   boxed, otherwise `none`. Unknown gives no affirmative permission. Marker
   composition replaces or clears earlier markers so a parent/sibling value
   cannot survive. Cover single/selected-single, panel member, sequence single
   and sequence panel member. The selected single must use `site_name` at spawn
   (`engine.rs:1059–1061`, beside `mark_delivery` at `1102`), not the outer phase
   mark in `seat_input`. Preserve the existing requested-input/spawn-time digest
   boundary and measure any resulting test pins. Boundary identity and HandsSpec
   composition read the same owned state; an unknown site cannot qualify a resume
   instance by being presented as known no-hands.

   In `adapters.rs::resume_gate`, keep absent/unsupported assessment and missing
   accounting refusals as `unsupported-resume`. For otherwise supported evidence,
   require both confinement markers as recognized strings before enablement:
   missing boundary alone, missing hands alone, both missing, null, non-string or
   unknown vocabulary declines `restrictions-unavailable`. Remove both permissive
   defaults. Retain identity, boundary/hands applicability, accounting, argv,
   exact-root and current-restriction checks. Unknown or namespace/boxed Codex
   never acquires work-site support; confirmed no-hands/harness controls stay live.

**Explicit council reconciliation and answered questions.**

| Claim / question | Disposition and evidence |
|---|---|
| Both: one `SiteFacts`, one structural move, drain `x`/`checks:x` first. Robustness R1/R5 and Q2. | Adopt the value move. Reject the old five-map checklist and robustness's array-of-maps fallback: neither makes a sixth field follow by construction. The two-line advice is explicitly withdrawn by simplicity and is not restored. |
| Robustness R2/R3, Q1: retain positive registration. Simplicity: no new Bundle field, project facts back out and mark every label via `site_boundary`. | Adopt the canonical table on `Bundle`, replacing authoritative sparse-map readers; reject that restriction on its internal representation. `site_boundary` currently derives None from hands-map absence, so unconditional marking would affirm the exact missing-field defect. Require resolved no-hands within the owner; registration without resolved confinement is also unknown. |
| Robustness Q3: thread raw owners or compile first. Simplicity: a dedicated pre-wrapper walk. | Combine a pre-collection structural registration walk with owner-aware population. Compiling first is acceptable only before any evidence write; the current pin collector writes too early and skips agent leaves, so it alone is not an ownership census. |
| Robustness R4: claim-time refusal. Simplicity: final-only refusal makes a transient overwrite harmless. | Adopt claim-time refusal plus the global final walk. Reject transient overwrite: raw collisions may disappear after wrapping and have already mixed facts. Reserve factless and deterministic owners before writes, including `verify:dialect-verify`, omitted from the resume walk. |
| Both: same-owner merges; no new persisted coordinates, structural manifest re-key, engine label fallback or provider branch. | Adopt. Preserve manifest grammar and site/instance digest vocabulary. Corrected contents may move digests. Equal evidence from distinct owners never permits aliasing. |
| Simplicity: mark the selected single at its executing label. Robustness: registration grants no capability. | Combine. Clear inherited markers and use resolved facts for that actual label at all dispatch paths. Validators and gate-class model sites remain offerless. |
| Robustness Q4: coverage cost versus staged-map fallback. | Accept the production-line cost of the enforced invariant. Equality is unchanged; cover every new path. Reducing lines by restoring partial relocation or permissive absence is refused. Missing tooling/host results are pending evidence, not a reason to weaken safety. |
| Both: decision/removal proofs; robustness's witness map assertion. | Adopt decisions and compiling controls; strengthen the witness control per Y to old-root refusal at the real eligibility/adapter decision with another provider present. Pin/map assertions are supplementary, never the sole proof. |
| Both: F3 test-only portability; simplicity: existing `sh -c` suffices. | Adopt a real executable or `#[cfg(unix)]` around direct-shebang tests. `sh` is a real executable only where available; it is not assumed installed on every Windows host. No production shell-interpreting fallback is permitted. Windows/macOS success awaits CI. |
| Simplicity: amend proposed 0056, do not create a decision; reject heading-count tests and broader re-keying. | Adopt. Before semantic implementation, minimally clarify 0056 rulings 1/6 for whole-family ownership and affirmative confinement, retaining `proposed`. This design-only artifact commit adds no decision file, count test or unrelated refactor. |

##### Scenario: Every whole-family shape reaches the actual Codex decision

- **GIVEN** real compilation of the shipped assessment, valid pinned inline
  argv and work-class single, hands-bearing panel member and no-hands panel
  member shapes, each wrapped and unwrapped
- **WHEN** a deterministic provider confirms a cold root and the engine produces
  the actual eligible retry offer/context at that executing coordinate
- **THEN** the supported single/no-hands controls execute `exec resume`, confirm
  exactly the offered root, re-impose current sandbox/effort and record
  `launch: resumed` without `unsupported-resume`
- **AND** the namespace/boxed hands-bearing member declines
  `restrictions-unavailable` and never resumes a work-site assessment; unconfirmed
  roots cannot produce a successful resumed launch
- **AND** panel `x` beside hands-bearing `checks:x` preserves both outcomes,
  a member called `checks` relocates by ancestry, and a real independent
  `verify:foo` phase remains at its own address; validators/gates get no offer.

##### Scenario: Unknown never supplies an affirmative no-hands fact

- **GIVEN** an otherwise supported assessment and an executing site with unknown
  registration or unresolved hands, or an adapter input missing boundary alone,
  hands alone, both markers, or containing null/non-string/unrecognized markers
- **WHEN** production engine composition and the production adapter gate judge
  the offer, without a test repairing either marker
- **THEN** no enabled work-site assessment or resumed launch follows; the
  adapter's unknown-marker cases decline `restrictions-unavailable`
- **AND** an absent/unsupported assessment declines `unsupported-resume`, while
  a registered, resolved no-hands control carries explicit markers and rejoins.

##### Scenario: Raw and wrapper-created ownership refuse before dispatch

- **GIVEN** a literal `verify:checks` exec phase with hands and no assessment
  beside wrapped no-hands Codex, or a literal Codex `verify:dialect-verify` beside
  the injected validator, or a literal member-destination phase
- **WHEN** distinct structural owners claim the same full executing label
- **THEN** compilation refuses with both owners and the label even if an owner
  has no populated facts; reversing declaration order does not change refusal
- **AND** raw phase/case collisions (`work`/`chore` versus `work:chore`), legal
  selector collisions (`work`, `chore`, `engine:stage` versus `work:chore`,
  `engine`, `stage`), default/literal-phase and raw aliases later separated by
  wrapping also refuse; legal selector defaults/results/pins are supplied
- **AND** renamed controls compile and a supported Codex retry remains live;
  ordinary repeated names and same-owner effort/resume evidence remain legal.

These refine Y's existing scenarios, not the delta inventory or task ledger.
Extend the existing runtime, bundle, adapter and CLI conformance suites. F1's
six forms must bridge actual engine cold/confirmed-root retry composition to
production Codex admission and launch. An engine transport seam may capture
Start/Resume and pass them unchanged into the real adapter exchange before the
boxed validator executes; it must retain the compiled wrapper and use durable
confirmed-root evidence for the retry. Do not inject assessments, guessed offers,
repaired markers or a simplified topology. Provider shims are deterministic
engineering evidence, not live qualification. Keep F1/F2 decisions runnable in
the box; #286 does not excuse their absence. Namespace creation itself remains
the controller's boundary-coverage obligation.

**Removal proof for every new test.** Keep each targeted mutation compiling;
record the exact diff, named behavioral assertion failure, restored bytes and
passing rerun. Disable the whole relocation to make supported wrapped decisions
fail, then use temporary incomplete-field mutations to expose the hands-bearing
and mirror cases without adding a partial-move production path. Independently
break affirmative marker production and the adapter absence guard; the latter
must turn an otherwise supported unknown-marker decision into enablement, so an
absent assessment cannot mask it. Mutate witness propagation so a changed copied
Codex declaration with another provider present permits an old root incorrectly;
the test must catch the lost identity refusal, not only a manifest difference.
Break raw and wrapper/final ownership protection for their respective refusal
cases, removing the minimal complete protection where guards overlap. A compile
error, earlier map lookup panic or unrelated failure is not removal proof. The
unwrapped/portable controls likewise need their claimed decision/launch mutation,
not a Linux run mislabeled as a Windows test. Commit no mutation.

F1 remains priority, then F2, then F3. Whole-family movement and the independent
guard form a coherent repair; partial relocation is not adoptable progress.
If capacity ends, implementation reports `oversized` with what remains, never
`broken` or unsafe enablement. This design office uses its own `drafted`/
`upstream` contract. Implementation, removal results, measured coverage and
remote platform signals remain evidence obligations; none is completed by this
reconciliation or by the inherited passing baseline.

and simplicity SHA-256

return cannot be confused with the earlier same-run positions above.

**Evidence and returned finding.** Clarification found no unanswered choice;
its clear result certifies no runtime repair. Source at `e8b99c5` confirms
`SiteFacts` and `HandsState`, the independent absent-marker refusal, and the
final structural walk including deterministic leaves. It also confirms the
missing pre-collection census (`assemble` seeds facts first), global-prefix
relocation with unchecked insertion (`relocate_verify_facts`), projection reads
in the engine, and missing selected-single marking beside `mark_delivery`.
Robustness additionally reports a temporary, restored probe: a DSH verify panel
member `alpha` beside a literal Codex phase `verify:alpha` compiled, transferring
the literal's Codex assessment/pin to `verify:checks:alpha`. The final labels
differ, so the final walk misses the raw alias. This chief corroborates its
source path, not a fresh execution of the probe. The probe observed mixed
facts; source tracing predicts a wrong refusal from the differing shape names,
not demonstrated enablement. A shape-name mismatch cannot substitute for
refusal before collection.

**Disposition of every claim and question.** These finish the existing D10
mechanism and task clauses 2–4; they introduce no replacement architecture.

| Council claim / question | Decision, evidence and alternative rejected |
|---|---|
| Both endorse the landed one-value family, positive no-hands registration and adapter absence guard (robustness R1/R2, simplicity F1). | Adopt. Keep `SiteFacts`, `HandsState::{Unknown, NoHands, Hands}` and `resume_gate`'s independent refusal. A sixth family member must follow the value. Reject five-map moves, empty-map permission and rollback to the reverted patch. These foundations alone are not F1 proof. |
| Both reject the prefix sweep (R1, simplicity A). | Adopt exact source/destination pairs from the parsed single or panel being wrapped. Enumerate its owners, drain their facts AND source reservations before any destination claim, then move whole values. Keep the `x`/`checks:x` overlap legal; an independent `verify:foo` remains untouched. A missing source stays unknown. Reject a global prefix scan or an engine `:checks` fallback. |
| Both require the raw census (R4, simplicity C); robustness Q1 asks whether it was deliberately dropped. | It was required and remains required; its omission is an implementation gap. Traverse all resolved authoring seats before `enforce_model_pins`, with agent/inline and factless leaves, case/default and step/member ancestry. `collect_unpinned` alone is insufficient because it skips agents. Use the existing structural `SiteKey` representation in a compilation-only label-to-owner census. Bind every fact write to its reservation; keep same-owner merges. Reject final-only checking: the reproduced raw alias disappears after wrapping. |
| Both require destination claims (R1/R4, simplicity D) and retain the final walk. | Adopt. After staging exact sources, claim every destination and injected-validator label against the remaining census before any destination/synthetic fact write. Refuse a distinct owner with the label and both structural descriptions. Keep `refuse_global_aliasing`, deterministic `owner_sites` and useful within-body diagnostics as a final independent check. Reject transient overwrite even if a later check returns an error. That compilation census supplies ownership; no new coordinate type, persisted owner field or second runtime facts table is needed. |
| Robustness R3/Q2 requires canonical security readers; simplicity favors keeping the landed projections. | Combine only where safe: keep existing manifest serialization and its vocabulary, but route runtime hands, boundary/delivery, spawn composition and inline assessment fallback through `Bundle.sites`. Preserve the selected candidate's assessment precedence for agent sites. At present projections are rebuilt correctly, so their mere existence proves no extra exploit; runtime dependence nevertheless violates D10's one-authority rule. Do not confuse `hands_spec() == None` with positive no-hands: identity/eligibility and markers must retain the tri-state distinction. Reject a runtime projection fallback, manifest re-key or digest-vocabulary change. |
| Both require selected-single marking (robustness §2.D/Q3, simplicity B). | A work-class selector single is already supported by SR1/0056, so retain its offer capability. Compose both markers from executing `site_name` beside `mark_delivery`, after the requested-input digest check, replacing parent markers. Unknown remains null/refused. Reject removing offers or accepting a silently cold supported case; the panel/sequence paths already demonstrate the executing-label pattern. |
| Both require six F1 decisions, F2/live controls, identity and removal proofs (robustness §4, simplicity tests). | Adopt all existing obligations; maps/offers alone are insufficient. The raw-alias refusal uses different provider kinds, with own-pin/assessment checks on its renamed control because a refused bundle has no compiled table to inspect. That control must still reach Codex's confirmed-root rejoin. Unknown-marker tests use otherwise supported/accounted evidence; missing/unsupported assessment retains `unsupported-resume`, then invalid confinement yields `restrictions-unavailable`. No unconfirmed, unknown or namespace/boxed work-site rejoin is enabled. |
| Both propose targeted removal; robustness §4.2 says deleting the final walk must expose wrapper aliases. | Adopt behavioral removal, reject that single-guard recipe once claim-time protection also exists. Remove the minimal complete overlapping protection for the admission test; separately exercise claim-time refusal before a write. A still-active guard masking a mutation, map panic or compile error is not proof. Each new test needs the named behavioral failure, exact restoration and green rerun. Keep independent adapter refusal effective when relocation is absent. |
| Both constrain F3; robustness R5 calls `sh -c` an existing pattern. | Adopt Unix guards or real target-platform executables. Existing `driver_conformance.rs` is Unix-only and `process.rs` calls `Command` directly; neither establishes that Windows supplies `sh`. Reject an unconditional shell/shim assumption or production shell fallback. F3 stays test-only; first native Windows/macOS evidence belongs to controller CI. |
| Robustness Q4 asks what to defer for coverage; both reject larger scope. | Defer no safety obligation in this design. All required production lines remain in the unchanged exact gate; capacity exhaustion reports `oversized` with the remainder, never partial relocation or unsafe enablement. Retain proposed 0056's landed clarification. Reject a new decision, public address format, broader map redesign, heading-count tests, task rewrite/ticks, frozen edits, C/D, archive and living-spec folds. |

##### Scenario: Wrapping cannot hide a raw authoring collision

- **GIVEN** a wrapped verify panel with pinned DSH member `alpha` and a distinct
  literal pinned Codex phase `verify:alpha`, with otherwise legal configuration
- **WHEN** the pre-collection census registers the two structural owners
- **THEN** compilation refuses `verify:alpha` and names both owners before pins,
  assessments or hands are written, even though final labels would differ
- **AND** renaming only the literal phase permits compilation; the member keeps
  its own driver evidence and the supported Codex phase's confirmed retry reaches
  `launch: resumed` with its own restrictions and declaration identity
- **AND** reversing declaration order does not change either outcome; removing
  the raw protection must fail the refusal assertion, not an unrelated check.

##### Scenario: A selected single publishes its own confinement at dispatch

- **GIVEN** a supported no-hands work-class single selected at `work:chore`,
  compiled with the shipped Codex assessment and a durably confirmed cold root
- **WHEN** the engine composes the actual retry and production adapter exchange
- **THEN** that case supplies explicit `not applicable`/`none`, rejoins exactly
  the offered root with current restrictions and records `launch: resumed`
- **AND** an unregistered or unresolved executing owner clears inherited markers
  and cannot qualify as known no-hands or enable the otherwise supported shape;
  adapter admission declines `restrictions-unavailable`
- **AND** removing the executing-label marker composition makes the supported
  decision fail; neither the harness nor a parent marker repairs the input.

These are design scenarios refining existing SR1/AS1/LE5, not new delta headings.
D11's six-form, identity and refusal bindings and the existing tasks clauses 2
(census), 3 (exact movement/claims/canonical readers/selected singles) and 4
(decisions/removal) already cover the complete reconciliation. Tasks remain
unchanged for that reason, not because implementation or proof is complete.
The landed proposed-0056 clarification is adopted. No new test, runtime fix,
removal result or coverage pass is established by this return.

#### THE PROOFS council reconciliation — 2026-09-16, adopted `614abde`

Read both positions in full. Robustness SHA-256:

analyses, not observed provider decisions, mutation results or gate runs.
This sitting applies AA to the settled D10 mechanism; it neither replaces that
mechanism nor adopts the earlier implementation gaps as a new work list.

**Council dispositions and answered questions.**

| Claim / question | Disposition, evidence and rejected alternative |

Neither alone connects the compiled shape to the real gate. Combine the existing compiler/engine and provider harnesses, with the controls below. No map-only substitute. |

Real adapter responses must reach the engine's normal checkpoint/store path before it computes retry. | Robustness: establish a confirmed root and assert `cold` plus a token for every row.
